[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 1/60]
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge

         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];

      end

      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row
         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col
            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe

               .rst        (rst),
               .ld         (ld),
               .en         (en),
               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),
               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),
               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),
               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),
               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),
               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]
I've studied the starting point. Key observations:

- **PE(i,j)** holds weight B[i][j], multiplies the streaming activation, accumulates partial sums southward; activations hop east through registers.
- Timing: `c_bot[j](t) = C[t-8-j][j]`, de-skew delay (7-j) → result row at t = m+15, matching the 15-stage valid pipe.
- All state advances only when `~w_valid` (compute enable).

**Optimization plan:**
1. Baseline eval of `starting_point.v` for calibration.
2. **v2**: exact 19-bit internal accumulator (8 products of 8×8 signed fit in ±2^17), gated clocks (kills all enable muxes), drop unused last-column east register, sign-extend at output only.
3. **v3**: carry-save accumulation (CSA instead of 19-bit carry-propagate add per PE; single final CPA per column) to shorten the critical path.

Let me create v2 and v3 and get the baseline cost.
  -> run_evaluation(filename='starting_point.v')
  -> create_file('{"content":"//#############################################################################\\n// tpu -- 8x8 weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU).\\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench \'tpu\' benchmark.\\n//\\n// Area/delay optimizations vs the reference RTL:\\n//   * exact 19-bit accumulator datapath (a sum of eight 8x8 signed products\\n//     is bounded by +-2^17); results are sign-extended to 32 bit only at the\\n//     output pins.\\n//   * both register domains run on gated clocks (the compute domain stalls\\n//     while weight rows shift), so no per-register clock-enable muxes exist.\\n//   * the unused east-hop register of the last column is removed.\\n//\\n// Timing contract (identical to the original):\\n//   weights shift south while w_valid (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]);\\n//   PE(i,j) sees activation A[t-i-j][i], column j leaves the bottom as\\n//   C[t-8-j][j], the de-skew adds N-1-j cycles, so result row m appears with\\n//   c_valid exactly 15 compute cycles after its A row was accepted.\\n//#############################################################################\\n\\nmodule tpu (\\n    input  wire         clk,\\n    input  wire         rst,\\n    input  wire         w_valid,\\n    input  wire [63:0]  w_data,\\n    input  wire         a_valid,\\n    input  wire [63:0]  a_data,\\n    output wire         c_valid,\\n    output wire [255:0] c_data\\n);\\n\\n   localparam N    = 8;\\n   localparam DW   = 8;\\n   localparam ACCW = 32;\\n   localparam W    = 19;     // weight-load clock\\n\\n   //#######################################################################\\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles.\\n   // a_row[i](t) = A[t-i][i]; Results stay 19-bit here and are sign-extended to')

Baseline: **4,624,409** (area 4604, delay 1004 ps). The critical path runs through the full 32-bit psum chain across 8 PEs. Now let me write v2 with gated clocks and exact-width accumulators.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench 'tpu' benchmark.\n//\n// Architecture: identical systolic data flow (activations hop west->east,\n// partial sums accumulate north->south, weights load top-down), but:\n//   - explicit gated clocks (no enable muxes anywhere),\n//   - exact-width internal accumulators (8 signed 8x8 products fit in\n//     [-2^17, 2^17), so 19 bits; 32-bit sign extension happens once,\n//     at the output),\n//   - the unused east register of the last PE column is dropped.\n//\n// Timing contract (unchanged):\n//   - ld (w_valid) shifts weights south; after 8 cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].\n//   - With ~ld the array advances: result row m appears 15 cycles after its\n//     A row was accepted. All state stalls while w_valid is high.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // async, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT  = 2*N - 1;\t // 15 compute cycles injection->output\n   localparam AW   = 19;\t\t // exact accumulator width (unused bits\n\t\t\t\t\t // of the 32-bit output stay zero: max\n\t\t\t\t\t // |sum of 8 s8*s8| < 2^17)\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of A delayed by i compute cycles; zeros when a_valid low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               else if (en)\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= (k == 0) ? din : sr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Weight shift registers (per column, load from top) + activation hop\n   // registers + accumulators. Gated clocks implement the stall semantics.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0]  w_cols;  // current stationary weight values\n   wire [N*AW-1:0]  c_bot;   // column accumulators (19-bit signed)\n   reg  [N*DW-1:0]  w_hold;  // weight storage\n   wire [N*AW-1:0]  acc_nxt;\n   wire [N*AW-1:0]  acc_q;\n   wire [N*DW-1:0]  a_hop;   // activations after one eastward hop\n\n   wire gclk = clk & en;\t  // gated clock: array advances only when ~ld\n   wire glk  = clk & ld;\t  // gated clock: weight load phase\n\n   // weight columns register\n   wire [DW-1:0] wv [0:N-1];\n   genvar\t   j;\n   for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_w\n      wire [DW-1:0] wout;\n      tpu_wreg #(.DW(DW)) u_wr\n\t(.clk(glk), .rst(rst), .d(w_data[j*DW +: DW]), .q(wout));\n      assign wv[j] = wout;\n   end\n   // q of the whole weight register file (kept one cycle behind the shift\n   // chain during load, exactly like the original: PE(i,j) sees the value\n   // that entered the top i cycles earlier)\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] w_all;\n   for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wv\n      assign w_all[j*DW +: DW] = wv[j];\n   end\n\n   // core (one unrolled layer of the systolic mesh, then re-circulated by the\n   // single activation hop register and the accumulators -- this is an exact\n   // behavioral rewrite of the PE mesh: a hop register + weight per cell)\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_row = a_left;\t\t// activations entering the mesh\n\n   // products of the current activations against the stationary weights\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] acc_in;\n\n   // one east hop for the activations (they must survive to the next cycle)\n   reg  [N*DW-1:0] a_east;\n\n   // accumulate: acc + prod(activation, weight) per column\n   genvar\t   c;\n   for (c = 0; c < N; c = c + 1) begin : g_acc\n      wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_row[c*DW +: DW]) * $signed(wv[c]);\n      assign acc_in[c*AW +: AW] =\n\t$signed(acc_q[c*AW +: AW]) + $signed(prod);\n   end\n\n   always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         acc_q    <= {N*AW{1'b0}};\n         a_east   <= {N*DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         acc_q  <= acc_in;\n         a_east <= a_row;\n      end\n   end\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew + sign extension + valid pipe.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar\t   jj;\n   generate\n      for (jj = 0; jj < N; jj = jj + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - jj;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = acc_q[jj*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[jj*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              {{(ACCW-AW){cin[AW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               else if (en)\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= (k == 0) ? cin : dr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign c_data[jj*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              {{(ACCW-AW){dr[D-1][AW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 15 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n\n// weight load shift register (one column); after N load cycles holds B[i][j]\nmodule tpu_wreg\n  #(parameter DW = 8)\n   (input\t clk,\n    input\t rst,\n    input [DW-1:0] d,\n    output [DW-1:0] q);\n   reg [DW-1:0] r;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n     if (rst) r <= {DW{1'b0}};\n     else r <= d;\n   assign q = r;\nendmodule\n", filename='v2.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passes with cost **4,624,409** (area 4604, delay 1004 ps). The critical path is per-PE: weight FF → multiplier → 32-bit add → psum FF, plus enable-mux logic. My optimization plan:

1. **v2**: exact-width 19-bit accumulators (8 signed 8×8 products fit in ±2^17), gated clocks to remove all enable muxes, drop the unused last-column east register, sign-extend only at the output.
2. **v3**: carry-save accumulation — keep (sum,carry) per PE, compress with a CSA, single carry-propagate add per column.

Note: my first v2 draft incorrectly collapsed the 8 rows into one multiplier row — that's mathematically wrong (each column must accumulate 8 distinct weight-index products). I'll write the full 64-PE structure properly.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench 'tpu' benchmark.\n//\n// Architecture: identical systolic data flow to the original (activations hop\n// west->east through registers, partial sums accumulate north->south, weights\n// shift south on load), with these optimizations:\n//   * explicit gated clocks  -> no enable feedback muxes anywhere\n//   * exact-width internal accumulators: a column result is a sum of 8 signed\n//     8x8 products, which lies in [-130048, 131072] => 19 bits suffice; the\n//     32-bit sign extension happens once, at the output pins\n//   * the unused east register of the last PE column is dropped\n//\n// Timing contract (identical to the original):\n//   * ld (w_valid) shifts weights south one row per cycle; after 8 load cycles\n//     PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].  Weight rows are pushed bottom-row first.\n//   * otherwise the array advances one compute cycle: result row m appears\n//     15 cycles after its A row was accepted (c_valid = a_valid delayed 15\n//     compute cycles).  All state stalls while w_valid is high.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // async, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15 compute cycles injection -> output\n   localparam AW  = 19;\t\t// exact accumulator width\n\n   wire ld   = w_valid;\n   wire en   = ~w_valid;\n   wire gclk = clk & en;\t// compute-phase gated clock\n   wire lclk = clk & ld;\t// weight-load gated clock\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles; zeros\n   // are injected while a_valid is low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               else\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= (k == 0) ? din : sr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (gated-clock PEs, 19-bit accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW), .AW(AW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .gclk(gclk), .lclk(lclk),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row leave together. 19-bit registers; sign extension once,\n   // at the output.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar\t   j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){cin[AW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               else\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= (k == 0) ? cin : dr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              {{(ACCW-AW){dr[D-1][AW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 15 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array core.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tgclk,\t// compute gated clock\n    input\t\tlclk,\t// load gated clock\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]   psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.rst        (rst),\n               .gclk       (gclk),\n               .lclk       (lclk),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Processing element. Two gated-clock domains:\n//   lclk (weight load): the column shift register.\n//   gclk (compute):     activation hop register + accumulator.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t  rst,\n    input\t\t  gclk,\n    input\t\t  lclk,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0]\t  psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // weight load shift register\n   always @(posedge lclk or posedge rst)\n     if (rst) w <= {DW{1'b0}};\n     else     w <= w_north;\n\n   always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         a_east     <= a_west;\n         psum_south <= psum_north +\n                       {{(AW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v2.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline (4.62M) passes; v2 (gated clocks + 19-bit accumulators) improves to **2.94M**. 

Key insight from analyzing the dataflow: the original's a-hop registers + output de-skew (980 FFs) are pure overhead — a cleaner architecture is a **pipelined dot-product tree per column**: delay all activation rows by K=12 cycles (shared 64-bit-wide delay line), then per column compute all 8 products simultaneously and sum through a 3-stage adder tree (L1: 4×17b, L2: 2×18b, L3: 19b out). Total latency: 12 (skew) + 3 (tree) = 15 ✓. This cuts registers from ~3011 to ~2280.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized weight-stationary matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench 'tpu' benchmark.\n//\n// Architecture: per-column pipelined dot-product tree.\n//\n//   C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]\n//\n// The original systolic array spreads the 8 products of one result row over\n// 8 cycles (per-PE partial-sum chain) and then de-skews the columns with\n// (N-1-j) extra pipeline stages.  Both effects are pure register overhead.\n// Instead we time-align all 8 activation rows of a tile row with a shared\n// K-cycle-deep delay line (K = 12), multiply against the stationary weights\n// and reduce each column with a 3-stage adder tree:\n//\n//     stage 1:  4 adders, pair sums of products   (17-bit)\n//     stage 2:  2 adders, pair sums of stage 1    (18-bit)\n//     stage 3:  1 adder, final column result      (19-bit, sign-extended\n//                once to the 32-bit output width)\n//\n// Latency: K (12) + 3 tree stages = 15 = 2*N-1 compute cycles, matching\n// c_valid = a_valid delayed by 15 compute cycles exactly.\n//\n// Stall semantics: while w_valid is high the array is frozen (gated clock)\n// and the weight shift registers advance one row per cycle (bottom matrix\n// row first), exactly like the original.  Zeros are injected while a_valid\n// is low.  All accumulators are exact: 8 signed 8x8 products lie in\n// [-130048, 131072], which fits signed 19 bits.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t  // async, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam K   = 12;\t\t// activation delay depth (LAT - tree stages)\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\t\t// exact accumulator width\n\n   wire ld   = w_valid;\n   wire en   = ~w_valid;\n   wire gclk = clk & en;\t// compute-phase gated clock\n   wire lclk = clk & ld;\t// weight-load gated clock\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Shared activation delay line: abar(t) = Arow(t-K), zeros while a_valid\n   // is low.  All columns consume the same time-aligned activation rows.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [N*DW-1:0] dly [0:K-1];\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_in = a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1'b0}};\n   integer\t k;\n   always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < K; k = k + 1)\n\t   dly[k] <= {N*DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t dly[0] <= a_in;\n\t for (k = 1; k < K; k = k + 1)\n\t   dly[k] <= dly[k-1];\n      end\n   end\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] abar = dly[K-1];\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Weight load: per-column shift register of depth N, fed from the top with\n   // the packed weight row (bottom matrix row first).  After N load cycles\n   // wq[i][j] = B[i][j].\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [DW-1:0] wq [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\t r, c;\n   always @(posedge lclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t for (r = 0; r < N; r = r + 1)\n\t   for (c = 0; c < N; c = c + 1)\n\t     wq[r][c] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t for (c = 0; c < N; c = c + 1) begin\n\t    wq[0][c] <= w_data[c*DW +: DW];\n\t    for (r = 1; r < N; r = r + 1)\n\t      wq[r][c] <= wq[r-1][c];\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Per-column pipelined dot-product tree.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar\t   j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire [16:0]\t  s1 [0:3];   // pair sums of products\n         reg  [16:0]\t  s1q [0:3];\n         wire [17:0]\t  s2 [0:1];   // pair sums of stage 1\n         reg  [17:0]\t  s2q [0:1];\n         wire [AW-1:0]\t  ot;\t      // final column result\n         reg  [AW-1:0]\t  oq;\n\n         genvar\t\t   g;\n         for (g = 0; g < 4; g = g + 1) begin : g_l1\n            wire [2*DW-1:0] pa = $signed(abar[(2*g)*DW +: DW]) *\n                                 $signed(wq[2*g][j]);\n            wire [2*DW-1:0] pb = $signed(abar[(2*g+1)*DW +: DW]) *\n                                 $signed(wq[2*g+1][j]);\n            // sign-extend the 16-bit products to 17 bits before adding\n            wire [16:0]\t   pae = {{(17-2*DW){pa[2*DW-1]}}, pa};\n            wire [16:0]\t   pbe = {{(17-2*DW){pb[2*DW-1]}}, pb};\n            assign s1[g] = pae + pbe;\n         end\n\n         wire [17:0] s2a = {{(18-17){s1q[0][16]}}, s1q[0]} +\n                           {{(18-17){s1q[1][16]}}, s1q[1]};\n         wire [17:0] s2b = {{(18-17){s1q[2][16]}}, s1q[2]} +\n                           {{(18-17){s1q[3][16]}}, s1q[3]};\n         assign s2[0] = s2a;\n         assign s2[1] = s2b;\n\n         wire [AW-1:0] ota = {{(AW-18){s2q[0][17]}}, s2q[0]} +\n                             {{(AW-18){s2q[1][17]}}, s2q[1]};\n         assign ot = ota;\n\n         always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n            if Best so far: 2938524.4456980876 area_delay_product (eval 2)

[ASSISTANT]
v2 passes at **2.94M** (area 3710, delay 792). The critical path is mult (≈334ps) + 19-bit carry-propagate add (≈370ps) in every PE.

**Key architectural insight:** the product for PE(i,j) of row m must sample the weight at cycle m+i+j (mid-drain reload semantics), so the systolic product schedule is fixed. But the *accumulation* can be redundant: store each column's running sum in **carry-save form (S,C)** — the per-PE add becomes a 3:2 CSA (~70ps instead of ~370ps), with one final CPA per column in the de-skew slack. Using a +2^14 bias per product keeps S/C at 19 bits unsigned (max accumulated value 2^18 < 2^19), and the final conversion is `V = S + C + 0x60000 (mod 2^19)`.

Let me build v4 and also check the vectors' head.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench 'tpu' benchmark.\n//\n// Systolic data flow is IDENTICAL to the original logikbench tpu:\n//   * weights shift south through per-column chains while w_valid is high\n//     (bottom matrix row first); after 8 load cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j],\n//   * activations hop west->east through one register per column,\n//   * row i of A is delayed i cycles at the input (skew), column j of the\n//     result is delayed N-1-j cycles at the output (de-skew),\n//   * the whole array advances only while w_valid is low (stall semantics),\n//   * result row m appears exactly 15 compute cycles after its A row.\n//\n// Micro-architecture optimizations:\n//   1. Gated clocks (clk & enable) instead of enable feedback muxes.\n//   2. Carry-save accumulation: each PE keeps its partial sum in redundant\n//      (sum, carry) form, so the per-PE add is a 3:2 carry-save adder\n//      instead of a 19-bit carry-propagate adder.  One carry-propagate\n//      adder per column converts back to two's complement, sitting in the\n//      output de-skew path.\n//   3. Exact-width accumulators: a column result is a sum of 8 signed 8x8\n//      products, which lies in [-130048, 131072]; 19 bits suffice.  Each\n//      product is biased by +2^14 so the redundant pair (S, C) can be held\n//      as UNSIGNED 19-bit words with S + C = true_sum + k*2^14 (k = number\n//      of products accumulated, max 8 => max value 2^18 < 2^19, so the\n//      carry-save addition never overflows and the discarded top carry is\n//      always zero).  The final per-column conversion is\n//          V = S + C + (2^19 - 2^17)  (mod 2^19)\n//      which yields the 19-bit two's-complement result; it is sign-extended\n//      to the 32-bit output width once, at the pins.\n//   4. The eastward activation register of the last PE column is unused and\n//      is removed.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // async, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t\t// 15 compute cycles injection -> output\n   localparam PW  = 19;\t\t\t// redundant accumulator word width\n   localparam [PW-1:0] CFIX = 19'h60000; // (2^19 - 2^17) mod 2^19\n\n   wire ld   = w_valid;\n   wire en   = ~w_valid;\n   wire gclk = clk & en;\t\t// compute gated clock\n   wire lclk = clk & ld;\t\t// weight-load gated clock\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles; zeros\n   // are injected while a_valid is low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               else\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= (k == 0) ? din : sr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array with carry-save accumulation.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\t// activation mesh (west -> east)\n   wire [PW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// redundant edge\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      // PE mesh\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            // stationary weight: shifts south on load edges\n            reg [DW-1:0] w;\n            always @(posedge lclk or posedge rst)\n              if (rst) assign a_h[i][j+1] = ae;\n            end\n\n            // local signed product, biased by +2^14 (16-bit pattern add)\n            wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_h[i][j]) * $signed(w);\n            wire [15:0]\t   pb   = {prod[15:14] + 2'b01, prod[13:0]};\n            wire [PW-1:0]   P    = {{(PW-16){1'b0}}, pb};\n\n            // 3:2 carry-save adder: (S, C, P) -> (S', C')\n            wire [ASSISTANT]
v4 passes at **2.69M** (delay 684). Now a key simplification: the per-product bias is unnecessary — carry-save addition preserves the sum **modulo 2^PW**, and the true column value ∈ [-130048, 131072] ⊂ [-2^18, 2^18), so with PW=19 the raw 19-bit words already yield the correct two's-complement pattern. Dropping the bias removes a 16-bit adder from every PE and the +CFIX from every output adder.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench 'tpu' benchmark.\n//\n// Systolic data flow is IDENTICAL to the original logikbench tpu:\n//   * weights shift south through per-column chains while w_valid is high\n//     (bottom matrix row first); after 8 load cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j],\n//   * activations hop west->east through one register per column,\n//   * row i of A is delayed i cycles at the input (skew), column j of the\n//     result is delayed N-1-j cycles at the output (de-skew),\n//   * the whole array advances only while w_valid is low (stall semantics),\n//   * result row m appears exactly 15 compute cycles after its A row was\n//     accepted (c_valid = a_valid delayed 15 compute cycles).\n//\n// Micro-architecture optimizations:\n//   1. Gated clocks (clk & enable) instead of enable feedback muxes.\n//   2. Carry-save accumulation: each PE keeps its partial sum in redundant\n//      (sum, carry) form, so the per-PE add is a 3:2 carry-save adder\n//      instead of a wide carry-propagate adder.  One 19-bit carry-propagate\n//      adder per column (in the output de-skew path) converts back to\n//      two's complement.\n//   3. Exact-width modular accumulation: carry-save addition preserves the\n//      multiset sum modulo 2^19, and every column result -- a sum of 8\n//      signed 8x8 products -- lies in [-130048, 131072] which is inside the\n//      19-bit two's-complement range [-2^18, 2^18).  So plain 19-bit words\n//      (no bias terms anywhere) reconstruct the exact result:\n//          V = S + C (mod 2^19), sign-extended to 32 bits at the pins.\n//   4. The eastward activation register of the last PE column is unused\n//      and is removed.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // async, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15 compute cycles injection -> output\n   localparam PW  = 19;\t\t// redundant accumulator word width\n\n   wire ld   = w_valid;\n   wire en   = ~w_valid;\n   wire gclk = clk & en;\t// compute gated clock\n   wire lclk = clk & ld;\t// weight-load gated clock\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles; zeros\n   // are injected while a_valid is low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               else\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= (k == 0) ? din : sr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array with carry-save accumulation.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\t// activation mesh (west -> east)\n   wire [PW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// redundant sum mesh (north -> south)\n   wire [PW-1:0] c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// redundant carry mesh\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// weight shift mesh (top -> south)\n\n   generate\n      // west edge / top edge\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      // PE mesh\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            // stationary weight: shifts south on load edges\n            reg [DW-1:0] w;\n            always @(posedge lclk or posedge rst)\n              if (rst) w <= {DW{1'b0}};\n              else     w <= w_v[i][j];\n            assign w_v[i+1][j] = w;\n\n            // activation hop register (unused in the last column)\n            if (j < N-1) begin : g_hop\n               reg [DW-1:0] ae;\n               always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst)\n                 if (rst) ae <= {DW{1'b0}};\n                 else     ae <= a_h[i][j];\n               assign a_h[i][j+1] = ae;\n            end\n\n            // local signed product, sign-extended to the accumulator width\n            wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_h[i][j]) * $signed(w);\n            wire [PW-1:0]   P    = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n            // 3:2 carry-save adder: (S, C, P) -> (S', C')\n            wire [PW-1:0] smaj = (s_v[i][j] & c_v[i][j]) |\n                                 (s_v[i][j] & P)       |\n                                 (c_v[i][j] & P);\n            reg [PW-1:0] sq, cq;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  sq <= {PW{1'b0}};\n                  cq <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else begin\n                  sq <= s_v[i][j] ^ c_v[i][j] ^ P;\n                  cq <= {smaj[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n               end\n            end\n            assign s_v[i+1][j] = sq;\n            assign c_v[i+1][j] = cq;\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Column result conversion (S + C -> two's complement) + output de-skew:\n   // column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so a result row leaves whole.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_out\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] val = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){val[PW-1]}}, val};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               else\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= (k == 0) ? val : dr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 15 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v5.v')
