[ASSISTANT]
v10 @ 400 = **1.742M** — new best. Now let me combine v10's 18-bit datapath with v8's 3-row accumulator idea (which failed before only because the old output stage was the bottleneck): 4 CSA levels in the PE instead of 5, with the 3-input speculative split at the output.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench 'tpu' benchmark.\n//\n// Systolic data flow is IDENTICAL to the original logikbench tpu:\n//   * weights shift south through per-column chains while w_valid is high\n//     (bottom matrix row first); after 8 load cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j],\n//   * activations hop west->east through one register per column,\n//   * row i of A is delayed i cycles at the input (skew), column j of the\n//     result is delayed N-1-j cycles at the output (de-skew),\n//   * the whole array advances only while w_valid is low (stall semantics),\n//   * result row m appears exactly 15 compute cycles after its A row was\n//     accepted (c_valid = a_valid delayed 15 compute cycles).\n//\n// Micro-architecture optimizations:\n//   1. Gated clocks (clk & enable) instead of enable feedback muxes.\n//   2. No per-PE carry-propagate multiplier.  Each PE computes its product\n//      as a Baugh-Wooley partial-product array (one AND/NAND level off the\n//      activation register):\n//          a*w = ARRAY - 2^15 + 2^8   (exact), so mod 2^PW\n//          a*w = ARRAY + (2^PW - 2^15) + 2^8.\n//   3. Three-row redundant accumulator (S, C, D): the per-PE tree reduces\n//      11 rows (3 accumulator + 8 partial-product rows) to 3 rows in only\n//      4 carry-save levels, and the third row bypasses the final level.\n//   4. 18-bit modular accumulation.  Every column result -- a sum of 8\n//      signed 8x8 products -- lies in [-130048, 131072].  In mod-2^18\n//      arithmetic the only ambiguous value is +131072 (it wraps to the\n//      single pattern 18'h20000; -131072 itself is unreachable because the\n//      true minimum is -130048).  The output stage repairs that one\n//      pattern, so 18-bit registers are exact:  V = S + C + D (mod 2^18).\n//   5. The output carry-propagate adder (3 rows -> 1) is pipelined across\n//      the output de-skew stages: column j (de-skew depth D = N-1-j >= 2)\n//      computes the speculative split add combinationally into de-skew\n//      stage 0 and shifts; column 6 (D = 1) registers the speculative sums\n//      and selects at the pins; column 7 (D = 0) uses a carry-save adder\n//      followed by a Kogge-Stone prefix adder straight to the pins.\n//   6. The eastward activation register of the last PE column is unused\n//      and is removed.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // async, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15 compute cycles injection -> output\n   localparam PW  = 18;\t\t// redundant accumulator word width\n\n   wire ld   = w_valid;\n   wire en   = ~w_valid;\n   wire gclk = clk & en;\t// compute gated clock\n   wire lclk = clk & ld;\t// weight-load gated clock\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles; zeros\n   // are injected while a_valid is low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               else\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= (k == 0) ? din : sr[k-1];\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array with 3-row carry-save multiply-accumulate.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\t// activation mesh (west -> east)\n   wire [PW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// accumulator row 1 (north -> south)\n   wire [PW-1:0] c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// accumulator row 2\n   wire [PW-1:0] d_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// accumulator row 3\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\t// weight shift mesh (top -> south)\n\n   generate\n      // west edge / top edge\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign d_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      // PE mesh\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            // activation hop register (unused in the last column)\n            if (j < N-1) begin : g_hop\n               reg [DW-1:0] ae;\n               always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst)\n                 if (rst) ae <= {DW{1'b0}};\n                 else     ae <= a_h[i][j];\n               assign a_h[i][j+1] = ae;\n            end\n\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n              (.rst     (rst),\n               .gclk    (gclk),\n               .lclk    (lclk),\n               .w_north (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west  (a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_in    (s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_in    (c_v[i][j]),\n               .d_in    (d_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .s_out   (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_out   (c_v[i+1][j]),\n               .d_out   (d_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output stage: carry-propagate conversion of the redundant triple\n   // (S, C, D) -> V = S + C + D (mod 2^18), pipelined across the de-skew\n   // chain so a result row leaves whole, 15 compute cycles after its A row,\n   // plus the +131072 extreme repair (V == 18'h20000 means the true value\n   // is +131072, which only that case can produce).\n   //\n   //   column j: de-skew depth D = N-1-j.\n   //   D >= 2 : the speculative split add feeds de-skew stage 0 directly;\n   //            stages 1..D-1 shift.\n   //   D == 1 : the speculative sums are registered; the select feeds the\n   //            output pins in the result cycle.\n   //   D == 0 : carry-save adder + Kogge-Stone prefix adder to the pins.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_out\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] s_last = s_v[N][j];\n         wire [PW-1:0] c_last = c_v[N][j];\n         wire [PW-1:0] d_last = d_v[N][j];\n\n         // speculative split of the 18-bit 3-input carry-propagate add:\n         // low 9 bits (carry-out is two bits, 3*511 = 1533 < 2^11) and the\n         // high 9 bits mod 2^9 for all three carry hypotheses\n         wire [10:0]\t  lo_f  = {2'b00, s_last[8:0]} + {2'b00, c_last[8:0]} +\n                                 {2'b00, d_last[8:0]};\n         wire [8:0]\t  hi0_f = s_last[PW-1:9] + c_last[PW-1:9] +\n                                 d_last[PW-1:9];\n         wire [8:0]\t  hi1_f = hi0_f + 1'b1;\n         wire [8:0]\t  hi2_f = hi0_f + 2'd2;\n\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            wire [PW-1:0] u  = s_last ^ c_last ^ d_last;\n            wire [PW-1:0] mj = (s_last & c_last) | (s_last & d_last) |\n                               (c_last & d_last);\n            wire [PW-1:0] cc = {mj[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n            wire [PW-1:0] ksv;\n            tpu_ks #(.W(PW)) u_ks (.x(u), .y(cc), .s(ksv));\n            wire\t  fx = ksv[PW-1] & ~|(ksv[PW-2:0]);\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              fx ? ({{(ACCW-PW){1'b0}}, 1'b1, {(PW-1){1'b0}}})\n\t\t : ({{(ACCW-PW){ksv[PW-1]}}, ksv});\n         end\n         else if (D == 1) begin : g_d1\n            reg [8:0] rlo;\n            reg [1:0] rk;\n            reg [8:0] rh0, rh1, rh2;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  rlo <= {9{1'b0}};\n                  rk  <= {2{1'b0}};\n                  rh0 <= {9{1'b0}};\n                  rh1 <= {9{1'b0}};\n                  rh2 <= {9{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else begin\n                  rlo <= lo_f[8:0];\n                  rk  <= lo_f[10:9];\n                  rh0 <= hi0_f;\n                  rh1 <= hi1_f;\n                  rh2 <= hi2_f;\n               end\n            end\n            wire [PW-1:0] vout = {(rk == 2'd2 ? rh2 :\n\t\t\t\t   rk == 2'd1 ? rh1 : rh0), rlo};\n            wire\t  fx   = vout[PW-1] & ~|(vout[PW-2:0]);\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              fx ? ({{(ACCW-PW){1'b0}}, 1'b1, {(PW-1){1'b0}}})\n\t\t : ({{(ACCW-PW){vout[PW-1]}}, vout});\n         end\n         else begin : g_d2p\n            // D >= 2: the speculative split add has slack, so de-skew\n            // stage 0 takes it combinationally; the chain is one stage\n            // deeper than the de-skew depth to keep the exact latency\n            wire [8:0] hi_c = (lo_f[10:9] == 2'd2) ? hi2_f :\n\t\t\t      (lo_f[10:9] == 2'd1) ? hi1_f : hi0_f;\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer\t k;\n            always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst)\n                 for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                   dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               else begin\n                  dr[0] <= {hi_c, lo_f[8:0]};\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            wire [PW-1:0] vout = dr[D-1];\n            wire\t  fx   = vout[PW-1] & ~|(vout[PW-2:0]);\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              fx ? ({{(ACCW-PW){1'b0}}, 1'b1, {(PW-1){1'b0}}})\n\t\t : ({{(ACCW-PW){vout[PW-1]}}, vout});\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 15 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Processing element: redundant multiply-accumulate cell.\n//\n//   (S,C,D) += a*w   (mod 2^PW), all non-clock logic carry-save.\n//\n// Weight load on lclk (column shift register); accumulate on gclk.\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 18)\n   (\n    input\t\t  rst,\n    input\t\t  gclk,\n    input\t\t  lclk,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  s_in,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  c_in,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  d_in,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  s_out,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  c_out,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  d_out\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight: shifts south on load edges\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   always @(posedge lclk or posedge rst)\n     if (rst) w <= {DW{1'b0}};\n     else     w <= w_north;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   //########################################################################\n   // Baugh-Wooley partial products of the signed 8x8 product, as PW-bit\n   // rows.  With a_i/w_j the two's-complement bits (bit 7 = sign):\n   //\n   //   a*w = ARRAY - 2^15 + 2^8                       (exact identity)\n   //   ARRAY = sum_{i<=6,j<=6} a_i&w_j << (i+j)\n   //         + sum_{i<=6} !(a_i&w_7) << (i+7)\n   //         + sum_{j<=6} !(a_7&w_j) << (j+7)\n   //         + (a_7&w_7) << 14\n   //\n   // so mod 2^PW:  a*w = ARRAY + (2^PW - 2^15) + 2^8.\n   // Row 0 carries the constant bits (8, 15, 16, 17).\n   //########################################################################\n   reg [PW-1:0] V [0:7];\n   integer\t ii, jj;\n   always @* begin\n      for (ii = 0; ii < 8; ii = ii + 1) begin\n         V[ii] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         for (jj = 0; jj < 7; jj = jj + 1)\n           V[ii][ii+jj] = (ii < 7) ? (a_west[ii] & w[jj])\n\t\t\t\t   : ~(a_west[7] & w[jj]);\n         V[ii][ii+7]  = (ii < 7) ? ~(a_west[ii] & w[7])\n\t\t\t\t : (a_west[7] & w[7]);\n      end\n      V[0][8]  = 1'b1;   // +2^8\n      V[0][15] = 1'b1;   // +2^PW - 2^15\n      V[0][16] = 1'b1;\n      V[0][17] = 1'b1;\n   end\n\n   //########################################################################\n   // Carry-save reduction of the 11 rows {s_in, c_in, d_in, V0..V7} down to\n   // 3.  Every step is exact modulo 2^PW (the top carry is dropped), which\n   // is valid because the true accumulated value fits in 18 bits modulo\n   // the single +131072 ambiguity repaired at the output.\n   //########################################################################\n   // pass 1: 3 CSAs -> 8 rows\n   wire [PW-1:0] m1a = (V[0] & V[1]) | (V[0] & s_in) | (V[1] & s_in);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s1a = V[0] ^ V[1] ^ s_in;\n   wire [PW-1:0] k1a = {m1a[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   wire [PW-1:0] m1b = (V[2] & V[3]) | (V[2] & c_in) | (V[3] & c_in);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s1b = V[2] ^ V[3] ^ c_in;\n   wire [PW-1:0] k1b = {m1b[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   wire [PW-1:0] m1c = (V[4] & V[5]) | (V[4] & d_in) | (V[5] & d_in);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s1c = V[4] ^ V[5] ^ d_in;\n   wire [PW-1:0] k1c = {m1c[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   // rows: s1a k1a s1b k1b s1c k1c V[6] V[7]\n\n   // pass 2: 2 CSAs -> 6 rows\n   wire [PW-1:0] m2a = (s1a & k1a) | (s1a & s1b) | (k1a & s1b);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s2a = s1a ^ k1a ^ s1b;\n   wire [PW-1:0] k2a = {m2a[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   wire [PW-1:0] m2b = (k1b & s1c) | (k1b & k1c) | (s1c & k1c);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s2b = k1b ^ s1c ^ k1c;\n   wire [PW-1:0] k2b = {m2b[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   // rows: s2a k2a s2b k2b V[6] V[7]\n\n   // pass 3: 2 CSAs -> 4 rows\n   wire [PW-1:0] m3a = (s2a & k2a) | (s2a & s2b) | (k2a & s2b);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s3a = s2a ^ k2a ^ s2b;\n   wire [PW-1:0] k3a = {m3a[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   wire [PW-1:0] m3b = (k2b & V[6]) | (k2b & V[7]) | (V[6] & V[7]);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s3b = k2b ^ V[6] ^ V[7];\n   wire [PW-1:0] k3b = {m3b[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   // rows: s3a k3a s3b k3b\n\n   // pass 4: 1 CSA -> 3 rows\n   wire [PW-1:0] m4 = (s3a & k3a) | (s3a & s3b) | (k3a & s3b);\n   wire [PW-1:0] s4 = s3a ^ k3a ^ s3b;\n   wire [PW-1:0] k4 = {m4[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n   // rows: s4 k4 k3b\n\n   always @(posedge gclk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         s_out <= {PW{1'b0}};\n         c_out <= {PW{1'b0}};\n         d_out <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         s_out <= s4;\n         c_out <= k4;\n         d_out <= k3b;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n// W-bit Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix adder (x + y mod 2^W).\n//#############################################################################\nmodule tpu_ks\n  #(parameter W = 18)\n   (input [W-1:0] x,\n    input [W-1:0] y,\n    output [W-1:0] s);\n\n   wire [W-1:0] p0 = x ^ y;\n   wire [W-1:0] g0 = x & y;\n\n   // prefix levels: distance 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 (covers W <= 32)\n   wire [W-1:0] p1, g1, p2, g2, p3, g3, p4, g4, p5, g5;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < W; i = i + 1) begin : lv1\n         if (i == 0) begin : b0\n            assign p1[0] = p0[0];\n            assign g1[0] = g0[0];\n         end\n         else begin : bn\n            assign p1[i] = p0[i] & p0[i-1];\n            assign g1[i] = g0[i] | (p0[i] & g0[i-1]);\n         end\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < W; i = i + 1) begin : lv2\n         if (i < 2) begin : pass\n            assign p2[i] = p1[i];\n            assign g2[i] = g1[i];\n         end\n         else begin : black\n            assign p2[i] = p1[i] & p1[i-2];\n            assign g2[i] = g1[i] | (p1[i] & g1[i-2]);\n         end\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < W; i = i + 1) begin : lv3\n         if (i < 4) begin : pass\n            assign p3[i] = p2[i];\n            assign g3[i] = g2[i];\n         end\n         else begin : black\n            assign p3[i] = p2[i] & p2[i-4];\n            assign g3[i] = g2[i] | (p2[i] & g2[i-4]);\n         end\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < W; i = i + 1) begin : lv4\n         if (i < 8) begin : pass\n            assign p4[i] = p3[i];\n            assign g4[i] = g3[i];\n         end\n         else begin : black\n            assign p4[i] = p3[i] & p3[i-8];\n            assign g4[i] = g3[i] | (p3[i] & g3[i-8]);\n         end\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < W; i = i + 1) begin : lv5\n         if (i < 16) begin : pass\n            assign p5[i] = p4[i];\n            assign g5[i] = g4[i];\n         end\n         else begin : black\n            assign p5[i] = p4[i] & p4[i-16];\n            assign g5[i] = g4[i] | (p4[i] & g4[i-16]);\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // carry into bit i is g5[i-1]; g5[i] spans [max(0,i-31) .. i] = [0..i]\n   assign s[0] = p0[0];\n   for (i = 1; i < W; i = i + 1) begin : sumgen\n      assign s[i] = p0[i] ^ g5[i-1];\n   end\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v11.v')

[ASSISTANT]
**Final result: 1,701,507 area_delay_product** — `v11.v` at target_delay=410 (100% correctness, 6782/6782 checks) — a **2.7× improvement** over the original starting point (4,624,409).

## Summary of the optimization journey

| Design | Key idea | Cost | Delay | Area |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `starting_point.v` | original logikbench RTL | 4,624,409 | 1004 ps | 4604 |
| `v2` | gated clocks + exact 19-bit accumulators | 2,938,524 | 792 ps | 3710 |
| `v4` | per-PE carry-save accumulation (CSA vs 32-bit CPA) | 2,691,915 | 684 ps | 3934 |
| `v6` | Baugh-Wooley PP array merged into a 5-level CSA tree (no per-PE multiplier) | 2,120,966 | 548 ps | 3870 |
| `v7` | output CPA split speculatively across the de-skew slack + Kogge-Stone for col 7 | 1,950,535 → 1,833,501 @400 | 500 ps | 3898 |
| `v10` | 18-bit modular accumulation with single extreme-pattern repair | 1,850,140 → 1,742,213 @400 | 457 ps | 3810 |
| **`v11`** | **3-row redundant accumulator → 4 CSA levels; 18-bit; speculative 3-input split** | **1,701,507 @410** | **432 ps** | **3939** |

## Key architectural insights that drove the gains

1. **Cycle-accurate contract discovery** — the vectors revealed mid-drain weight reloads mix old/new weights per *individual* PE (PE(i,j) samples its weight at cycle m+i+j), which killed the shared-snapshot/tree approaches (v3) and forced keeping the true systolic schedule.

2. **No carry-propagate multiplier anywhere**: the signed 8×8 product is expressed exactly as a Baugh-Wooley partial-product array (`a*w = ARRAY − 2^15 + 2^8`), merged directly into the redundant accumulator with AND/NAND gates + CSA levels — this removed the ~300ps multiplier+adder chain from every PE.

3. **Redundant number system end-to-end**: each PE keeps (S,C) (or (S,C,D)) in carry-save form; the only carry-propagate adder per column sits in the output de-skew slack, split into a speculative 2-level low/high add whose select lands exactly on the de-skew depth that the original architecture already had.

4. **Modular exact-width accumulation**: since a column result (sum of 8 signed 8×8 products) lies in [−130048, +131072], 18-bit two's complement is exact except for the single wrappable value +131072 (pattern `18'h20000`, uniquely decodable since −131072 is unreachable) — repaired with 2 gates per column at the output pins.

5. **Gated clocks** (`clk & ~w_valid`) eliminated every enable feedback mux, and the unused east register of the last PE column was dropped.

## Final Summary

**Best design: `v11.v` @ target_delay=410 — 1,701,507 area_delay_product (PASS, 6782/6782 checks), 2.7× better than the starting point (4,624,409).**

### 1. Approaches tried
- **v2**: direct rewrite of the original systolic array with gated clocks + exact-width (19-bit) accumulators — correct, 2.94M.
- **v3**: replaced the systolic array with a shared-weights pipelined dot-product tree — **failed**: vectors contain mid-drain weight reloads, so PE(i,j) must sample its *current* weight at cycle m+i+j; the per-PE systolic schedule is contractual.
- **v4/v5**: kept the systolic schedule, converted per-PE accumulation to carry-save (S,C) form — 2.69M.
- **v6**: eliminated the per-PE multiplier entirely — expressed the signed 8×8 product as a Baugh-Wooley partial-product array (`a*w = ARRAY − 2^15 + 2^8`) merged directly into the accumulator via a 5-level CSA chain — 2.12M.
- **v7**: pipelined the output carry-propagate adder into the de-skew slack (speculative low/high split) + Kogge-Stone for the zero-delay column — 1.95M (1.83M @ target 400).
- **v10**: shrank to 18-bit modular accumulation with a single extreme-pattern repair at the pins — 1.74M.
- **v11** (winner): combined v10's 18-bit width with a 3-row accumulator (S,C,D) → only 4 CSA levels per PE.

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
1. **Baugh-Wooley PP array merged into the CSA tree** (no carry-propagate multiplier in any PE) — biggest single win (~570k).
2. **Redundant (carry-save) accumulation end-to-end**, with the only CPA hidden in the output de-skew slack (~740k cumulative).
3. **Exact-width modular arithmetic**: 18 bits suffice because a column sum ∈ [−130048, +131072]; only +131072 wraps, to the uniquely decodable pattern `18'h20000` (repaired with 2 gates/column) (~90k + smaller registers everywhere).
4. **Gated clocks** (`clk & ~w_valid`) removing all enable muxes (~1.7M total from the baseline).
5. **target_delay tuning** (500→410 ps) was worth ~3–5% each time.

### 3. What didn't work / regressions
- **v3** shared-weight tree: fundamentally incompatible with mid-stream weight reload semantics.
- **v8** (3-row accumulator at 19-bit with the old output stage): worse than v7 — extra 19-bit register outweighed the saved CSA level; only paid off once widths were cut to 18 bits (v11).
- Multiple bit-slicing bugs in the speculative output adders (wrong carry bit index, dropped top bits in concatenations) cost several eval steps — e.g. `{hi_c, lo_f[9:0]}` silently truncating `hi_c[8]`.

### 4. Lessons learned
- **Read the golden vectors early** — line-level diagnosis of failures (line 271: reload during drain; exact 0x400/0x40000 offsets: dropped carry bits) pinpointed bugs faster than re-reasoning from the spec.
- **The mathematics of range analysis pays directly**: proving min/max of accumulated sums let me cut 32→19→18 bits and justify dropped carries (mod-2^k CSA correctness).
- **Check what the synthesizer does with `$signed(*)`** — Yosys mapped it to a slow ripple chain; explicit PP generation was far better.
- Next time: I'd combine the width reduction (v10) with the 3-row tree (v11) immediately instead of testing them serially, and I'd hand-verify concatenation widths in speculative adders before evaluating.
